Mastiff Armor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] set unlocked at [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer IV (4)]]. This is specially designed to help take down the [[Sven Packmaster]], whose [[True Damage]] attacks completely ignore the player's [[Defense]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Mastiff Crown ([[Mastiff Armor]])|Wolf Slayer IV (4) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x Helmet of Growth ([[Armor of Growth]]) 4x [[Golden Tooth]] 64x [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] x16 ×4 + [[Armor of Growth]] → [[Mastiff Armor]]|
|Mastiff Chestplate ([[Mastiff Armor]])|Wolf Slayer IV (4) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x Chestplate of Growth ([[Armor of Growth]]) 4x [[Golden Tooth]] 64x [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] x16 ×4 + [[Armor of Growth]] → [[Mastiff Armor]]|
|Mastiff Leggings ([[Mastiff Armor]])|Wolf Slayer IV (4) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x Leggings of Growth ([[Armor of Growth]]) 4x [[Golden Tooth]] 64x [[Enchanted Diamond]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Diamond]] x16 ×4 + [[Armor of Growth]] → [[Mastiff Armor]]|
|Mastiff Boots ([[Mastiff Armor]])|Wolf Slayer IV (4) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x Boots of Growth ([[Armor of Growth]]) 4x [[Golden Tooth]] 64x [[Enchanted Diamond]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Diamond]] x16 ×4 + [[Armor of Growth]] → [[Mastiff Armor]]|

## Trivia
- Mastiff Armor, when paired with an old bugged version of the [[Blue Whale Pet]], could grant the player upwards of 80,000 [[Defense]].
- The ability of the Mastiff Armor's name, Absolute Unit, is based on the absolute unit meme, in which objects or animals of large size would be called an absolute unit.
- Despite the name of the armor, the armor set's appearance isn't based off an actual mastiff, but rather the Shiba Inu dog breed, most notably known for the Doge meme.
- Inside of dungeons, before Floor 7 released, it was possible to get upwards of 100 million EHP using a [[Rarity|Rare+]] [[Blue Whale Pet]] in dungeons due to each piece giving only -10,000 [[Defense]].
- Except for [[Artifact of Power]], [[Relic of Power]] and [[Shimmersparkle Chestplate]], Mastiff Armor pieces are tied for having the most gemstone slots with [[Armor of Divan]], [[Divan's Drill]] and [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].

# Mauve Skin

Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Item skins give your gear a fresh new look! Apply them just like an enchant book using the anvil.; This skin can be applied to Shadow Assassin Helmet and  Shadow Assassin Helmet.; EPIC COSMETIC
The Mauve Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Mauve Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on March 30, 2021. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and, unlike other Fire Sales, had an unlimited stock, with 4,340 sold.
## Usage
The Mauve Skin can be applied to a [[Shadow Assassin Armor|Shadow Assassin Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance.

The Mawdredge Dagger is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer IV (4)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Slayer IV (4) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] 1x [[Twilight Dagger]] 8x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] + [[Molten Powder]] x8 + [[Twilight Dagger]] → Mawdredge Dagger|
## Usage
Sword:
Mawdredge Dagger [[Damage]]: +130 [[Strength]]: +55 [[Crit Damage]]: +20 [[Attack Speed]]: +10
[Effects]
[Deal 2.5x damage to Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs.; Deal 1.5x damage to Skeletal mobs.; Gain +30 Combat Wisdom against Infernal (Infernal (Mob Type)) mobs. Ability: Attunement ON RIGHT CLICK; Swap between Spirit and Crystal mode in order to attack through Hellion Shields. Ability: Wraith Drain; On hit, heal for 200 + 4% Health after 6s if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement.]
The Mawdredge Dagger deals 2.5x damage to [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs and 1.5x damage to [[Skeletal]] mobs. Additionally, it provides +30 [[Combat Wisdom]] against [[Infernal (Mob Type)|Infernal]] mobs.
The Mawdredge Dagger has 2 abilities:
- Attunement allows the wielder to swap between Spirit and Crystal attunements in order to attack through the [[Inferno Demonlord]]'s Hellion Shield ability. When switching attunement, the Mawdredge Dagger changes appearance from a Iron Sword to a Golden Sword.
- Wraith Drain allows the wielder to heal 200 [[Health]] + 4% after 6s upon hit if the Hellion Shield has switched attunement.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Deathripper Dagger]]|Blaze Slayer VI (6) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|[[Mawdredge Dagger]] 1x Mawdredge Dagger 16x [[Inferno Vertex]] 16x [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] 64x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] x16 + [[Molten Powder]] x64 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + Mawdredge Dagger → [[Deathripper Dagger]]|

# McGrubber's Burger
Aliases: Mcgrubber
Categories: Epic items, Items, Confirmations needed, Slayer, Vampire Slayer
[McGrubber's Burger]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell (Motes): 30,000 [[Motes]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MCGRUBBER_BURGER
Tooltip: Consumable; Consume this tasty burger to permanently gain a Grubber Stack.; Each stack grants:; +12 Rift Time +5% Motes from Motes Grubber +40 Motes on Orb pickup Max 5 stacks!; Right-click to consume!; EPIC
McGrubber's Burger is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Consumables|Permanent Consumable]] item in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
McGrubber's Burger is a [[Drop Chance|7Extraordinary]] drop from Tier IV [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] or above once the player reaches [[Vampire Slayer|Vampire Slayer V (5)]].
### Drop Chances
- Tier IV: 1.22% (1 in 81.97 kills)
- Tier V: 1.2% (1 in 83.33 kills)
## Usage
The player can consume up to 5 McGrubber's Burgers, which each give a Grubber Stack, granting:
- +12 [[Rift Time]]
- +5% [[Motes]] from the [[Motes Grubber]]
- +40 [[Motes]] on [[Motes|Orb]] pickup
- [[SkyBlock Levels|+5 SkyBlock XP]]
In total, 5 Grubber Stack will grant:
- +60 [[Rift Time]]
- +25% [[Motes]] from the [[Motes Grubber]]
- +200 [[Motes]] on [[Motes|Orb]] pickup
- [[SkyBlock Levels|+25 SkyBlock XP]]
## Game Message
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Consuming; Less than 5 burgers consumed|YUM! You ate a McGrubber's Burger! You have # Grubber Stacks!|
|Consuming; 5 or more burgers consumed|You cannot eat more burgers! Please watch your cholesterol.|
## Tips
- It is recommended to grind McGrubber's Burgers using Tier 4 [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend|Riftstalker Bloodfiends]], as Tier Vs have a lower drop chance and are much more difficult even for experienced players, albeit they fill the [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]] by 25% more per boss.
  - When accounting for higher spawn requirements and boss HP, Tier 5s fill the [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]] about 3.33% slower than Tier IVs (assuming the boss takes about as long to kill as to spawn).
- While McGrubber's Burgers can't be traded or taken out from [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]], it is possible to reduce the grind required using [[Ananke Feather|Ananke Feathers]].
  - A total of 12 [[Ananke Feather|Ananke Feathers]] are required to guarantee [[McGrubber's Burger]] 5 McGrubber's Burgers within 5 bosses (Tier IV and above).
  - In a [[Co-op]], however, it is possible to share between members by dropping a McGrubber's Burger on the ground for the other to pick up.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Trivia
- The name McGrubber's Burger is most likely a reference to McDonald's, a worldwide fast food restaurant known for their burgers and fries.
- The effect doesn't affect the orbs in [[Enigma's Crib]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added McGrubber's Burger.|
|May 18, 2023|Removed +999 Motes every time you join the Rift and replaced it with +40 Motes on Orb pickup.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added McGrubber's Burger.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Added various SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) tasks for consumables, including Metaphysical Serums ([[Metaphysical Serum]]) and McGrubber's Burgers.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MELODY_HAIR
Tooltip: Intelligence: +6; Accessory Power: +12; A single strand of hair from Melody's head.; Left-Click: Tune up!; Right-Click: Play note!; ❣ Requires Melody Harp completion.; * Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
Melody's Hair is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] obtainable through [[Melody]]'s quest-line in [[The Park]].
## Obtaining
Upon completing the main Harp songs (up to La Vie en Rose – excluding the [[Personal Harp]] exclusive songs, Through the Campfire and Pachelbel) with at least a 90% score, [[Melody]] will reward you with this accessory.
## Usage
The accessory grants +6 [[Intelligence]].
Left-click the [[Accessories|Accessory]] to tune-up and right-click to play the note you tuned.
Melody's Hair has a range of 24 notes, over 2 octaves. From lowest to highest on the scale (Chromatic Scale), the following are the notes you can play:
F#, G, G#, A, A#, B, C, C#, D, D#, E, F, F#, G, G#, A, A#, B, C, C#, D, D#, E, F
## Trivia
- If you complete La Vie en Rose through the [[Personal Harp]], you still have to go talk to [[Melody]] in the [[Savanna Woodland]] to claim Melody's Hair.
- Previously you could trade with another player to get this [[Accessories|accessory]], the person receiving the item will not be able to use it if they have not already completed the final song (La Vie en Rose) and earned their own. None of the properties of the item will work either (the [[Intelligence]] boost, item ability, or [[Accessory Power]] obtained from the item).
  - Sometimes it is bugged and says "Requires Melody's Hair completion" even if you have completed La Vie en Rose. Switching to a different lobby usually resolves this.
- [[Experience Artifact]], [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactor]], [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]], [[Book of Progression]], Melody's Hair, [[Zombie Talisman]], [[Skeleton Talisman]], [[Archaeologist's Compass]], [[Lucky Hoof]], [[Eternal Hoof]], [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]] and [[Ring of Love|Yellow Rock of Love]] are the only accessories to use a vanilla sprite, rather than a custom made one.

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Snowman pets.; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; EPIC COSMETIC
The Melting Snowman Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Melting Snowman Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on January 25, 2025. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 7,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Melting Snowman Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Snowman Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

The Mender Crown is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] that grants +80 [[Mending]] and [[Vitality]] while in Dungeons. It is an upgraded version of the [[Mender Fedora]]. It requires [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] Completion for the player to use.
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Mender Crown can be bought from [[Ophelia]] for 1 [[Mender Fedora]] and 5,000,000 [[Coins]] after the player has completed [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].
## Usage

- The Mender line of helmets used to have different textures featuring the Red Cross emblem. They were changed because they were in violation of the Geneva Conventions.
## Tips
- The best [[Reforging|reforge]] for this is [[Reforging|Empowered]] as it also boosts healing.

The Mender Fedora is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Helmets|Dungeon Helmet]] that grants +65 [[Mending]] and [[Vitality]] while in [[Dungeons]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Mender Fedora requires the completion of [[The Catacombs - Floor III|Catacombs - Floor III]] to be purchased from [[Ophelia]] for 1 [[Mender Helmet]] and 800,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Helmet:
Mender Fedora [[Health]]: +220
[Effects]
[Grants +65 Mending and Vitality while in Dungeons.]
The Mender Fedora grants +65 [[Mending]] and [[Vitality]] to its wearer while in Dungeons. It is the direct upgrade of the [[Mender Helmet]], adding the previous healing bonus of it to also affect the wearer, with the next tier up being the [[Mender Crown]]. It requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs XVI (16)]] to be used.
## Essence Crafting

|[[Caduceus Mender Skin]]|[[Caduceus Mender Skin]]|ULTIMATE ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - November 11, 2023 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Trivia
- The Mender line of helmets used to have different textures featuring the Red Cross emblem. They were changed because they were in violation of the Geneva Conventions.
## Tips
- Possibly the best reforge for this helmet is [[Reforging|Giant]], as the large increase in HP outweighs the boosted healing from [[Reforging|Empowered]].
  - Alternatively, [[Reforging|Perfect]] can be used as a budget reforge.
  - Alternatively, running [[Reforging|Ancient]] or [[Reforging|Necrotic]] with a hybrid build may also benefit.

# Metaphysical Serum
Aliases: Serum, Soulfish
Categories: Epic items, Items, Permanent Consumables
[Metaphysical Serum]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: METAPHYSICAL_SERUM
Tooltip: Consumable; Drinking this serum shortens the chain required for Superpairs add-on experiments by 1!; You may drink up to 3 doses of the serum before the taste becomes too abstract.; Right-click to consume!; EPIC
Metaphysical Serum is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Consumables|Permanent Consumable]] item obtained rarely in [[Experiments|Superpairs]].
## Obtaining
Metaphysical Serum is a 1.0638% (162 in 15625) chance drop from Experiments, obtainable from Grand experiments or higher.
## Usage
The player can consume up to 3 Metaphysical Serums, which each give a permanent buff shortening the chain required in Superpairs add-on experiments by 1 and granting the player [[SkyBlock Levels|+5 SkyBlock XP]] up to [[SkyBlock Levels|+15 SkyBlock XP]] when all three are consumed.
Additionally, after consuming all three Metaphysical Serums, you will be rewarded with a permanent +2 clicks in Superpairs.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|First Serum|DELICIOUS! The wonderfully complex taste of the Metaphysical Serum has taken over all of your taste buds!|
|Second Serum|AMAZING? The Metaphysical Serum seems to have taken over half your brain, you should probably stop drinking more, seriously...|
|Third Serum|CONCERNING! The Metaphysical Serum has caused irreversible damage to your whole body- but hey, at least you got some SkyBlock XP in return! Bonus! You now get an extra 2 clicks on Superpairs!|
|Attempting to drink more than three|YUCKY! This serum seems to have lost it's flavor! (You've drank the max amount!)|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Added Metaphysical Serum.|

Applied To: [[Midas' Sword]] [[Midas Staff]]
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXV (25) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes

Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MIDAS_JEWEL
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Gilded reforge when combined with Midas Staff,  Midas Staff, Midas' Sword, and  Midas' Sword.; Midas' most prized possession - a gift from his older brother, whose boundless generosity got the better of him. While he always admired his brother, Midas swore his kingdom would not end the same way.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Damage  Strength  Ability Damage; Byron's Compassion (Gilded); Killing a mob has a rare chance to grant Coins to a nearby player.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 25.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
The Midas Jewel is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Gilded]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to the [[Midas' Sword]] and [[Midas Staff]], granting [[Damage]], [[Strength]] and [[Ability Damage]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv190-716]|[[King Midas]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
## Usage
### Reforging
Midas Jewel can be combined with a [[Midas' Sword]] and [[Midas Staff]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Gilded]] reforge, to grant [[Damage]], [[Strength]] and [[Ability Damage]], which scale with the Weapon's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXV (25)]]. A weapon that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Gilded]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Byron's Compassion ability, which gives the holder a 1% chance to grant [[Coins]] to a random player in the same lobby upon killing an enemy. This bonus can grant up to 50,000,000 [[Coins]]. This bonus does not affect [[Ironman]] players or players on the same Co-op.
|Amount given|Chance|
|-|-|
|100–1,000 [[Coins]]|74.99%|
|1,000–5,000 [[Coins]]|21.00%|
|5,000–50,000 [[Coins]]|3.10%|
|50,000–250,000 [[Coins]]|0.81%|
|250,000–1,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.08%|
|1,000,000–50,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.01%|
|Stat Bonus|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+8 AD ([[Ability Damage]]) +75 Str ([[Strength]]) +75 Dmg ([[Damage]])|+10 AD ([[Ability Damage]]) +90 Str ([[Strength]]) +90 Dmg ([[Damage]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Byron's Compassion (Gilded) Killing a mob has a rare chance to grant Coins to a nearby player.|Byron's Compassion (Gilded) Killing a mob has a rare chance to grant Coins to a nearby player.|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The Midas Jewel was the first item to be applicable to specific items only.
- Its description, alongside the name of the ability the reforge stone grants, implies that [[King Midas]]'s brother is called Byron.
- The name of Byron might be a reference to the musician Byron Lee, who made an LP named "The Midas Touch"
- Byron's Compassion provides an average of 61.5995 [[Coins]] per kill.
- It and the [[Warped Stone]] are the only [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that have no stat at [[Rarity|DIVINE]] rarity.

The Mimic Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Faker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Mimic Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Mimic]] with enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Mimic Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Mimic Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Faker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Faker ([[Attributes]]); Dungeon Blessings ([[Dungeon Blessing]]) are +1%–10% stronger on you.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Mimic Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Mimic Shard has the following description:
- These cunning creatures can spring to life in an instant, revealing sharp fangs and a ravenous appetite for anything that approaches.

### Tiered Bonus
Similar to the Efficient Miner perk in the [[Heart of the Mountain]] that grants [[Mining Spread]], [[Mineral Armor]] Mineral Armor grants extra [[Mining Spread]] on Ores and Blocks.
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Paladin Mineral Helmet Skin]]|[[Paladin Mineral Helmet Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Winter 2025 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin is swappable between 20 holy paladin variants!|
|[[Paladin Mineral Helmet Skin]]|[[Paladin Mineral Helmet Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|(same as above)||
|[[Royal Skin]]|[[Royal Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Fall 2024 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Trivia
- It was once possible to obtain a [[Armor|Dungeon Armor]] version of this set by turning the base [[Hardened Diamond Armor]] into a dungeon item via Essence Crafting.
- This set was best used for leveling up [[Mining]] by mining [[End Stone]] in the [[End Island|End]]. When using this method, it was best to use a [[Stonk]] so that [[Endermite|Endermites]] didn't spawn. This method could result in up to +600,000 [[Mining|Mining XP]] per hour. However, since the introduction of the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]], mining [[Mithril]] or completing [[Commissions]] is much more efficient.
- Although it isn't stated in the armor itself, the Mineral Armor special effect does not work on private islands.
- Mineral armor was added before Mining Spread was a stat.
## History

|Sep 28, 2022|0.15|Now works on [[Glowstone]].|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Mineral Armor no longer works on Pure Gold within Mines of Divan.|
|Unknown Date|Reverted changes to Mineral Armor on Pure Gold.|

# Minos Relic
Categories: Epic items, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Pet items, 0.7.8
[Minos Relic]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Hephaestus Relic]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 5,000,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MINOS_RELIC
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Increases all pet stats by 33.3%.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; EPIC PET ITEM
Minos Relic is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] obtained from [[Minos Champion|Minos Champions]]. It increases a [[Pets|Pet]]'s stats by 33.3%.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv285]|Empyrean Minos Champion ([[Minos Champion]])|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.04%|
|[Lv248]|Exalted Minos Champion ([[Minos Champion]])|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.03%|
|[Lv175]|Venerable Minos Champion ([[Minos Champion]])|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.02%|
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Minos Relic will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it increased base [[Stats]] by 33.3%. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Minos Relic for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hephaestus Relic]]|None|1x [[Hephaestus Anvil]] [[Minos Relic]] 3x Minos Relic|Recipe: Minos Relic ×3 + [[Hephaestus Anvil]] → [[Hephaestus Relic]]|
## Trivia
- The Minos Relic used to be commonly equipped with a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]] for its [[Farming Fortune]]. Since the addition of [[Green Bandana]], this is no longer worthwhile.
- The Minos Relic does not increase the perks of a pet, for example, a [[Black Cat Pet]], which has [[Magic Find]] as one of its perks, would not be increased by a Minos Relic.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Oct 16, 2025|Minos Relic can now be sold to NPCs.|
|Jan 20, 2026|0.24.1 RC|Minos Relic now only affects Combat Stats.|
|Jan 20, 2026|0.24.1 RC|Hephaestus Relic once again affects all stats, with the former change being delayed.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added Minos Relic.|

The Mithril Coat is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Chestplates|Chestplate]] unlocked at [[Mithril|Mithril VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Mithril VII (7) ([[Mithril]])|32x [[Enchanted Mithril]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mithril]] x4 ×8 → Mithril Coat|
## Usage
Chestplate:
Mithril Coat [[Defense]]: +125 [[Speed]]: +15
[Effects]
[Ability: Mithril's Protection; Any damage taken is max 40% of the wearer's Health. Gain Regeneration when this ability activates.]
## Tips
- This chestplate can be used to survive hits that deal unsurvivably high damage, including:
  - Master Mode Floor 2, to easily survive the Warrior's Charge during the boss fight.
  - Insta-killing attacks, such as the [[Voidgloom Seraph]]'s Yang Glyphs ability, [[Inferno Demonlord]]'s Fire Pillars and/or DDR Apocalypse, [[Kuudra|Dropships]]' Bomb [[Catacombs Mobs|Deathmites]] and [[Kalhuiki Tribe Member|Kalhuiki Tribe Members.]]
## Trivia
- The ability of this item doesn't work in [[Trials of Fire]].
- This item is most likely a reference to the Mithril-coat in Lord of the Rings.

Moody Grappleshot
[Effects]
[Ability: Big Pull RIGHT CLICK; Throw a hook up to 15 blocks to grab blazes and certain other Airborne mobs (up to 5 at the time).; Pulling a mob multiplies its damage taken by 2x and stops it from flying.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 30; Cooldown: 1s]
Big Pull works on every [[Blaze]] (except [[Inferno Demonlord]]), [[Torrid]] and [[Silkbreeze]].
## Trivia
- There was a bug that allowed players to drag a [[Millennia-Aged Blaze]] or [[Burningsoul Demon]] while warping to the Crimson Isle spawn, allowing them to spawn-kill players.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MORNING_DEW
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Majestic reforge when combined with Safari Belt.; That, too, soon fades away, along with a single tear, like morning dew.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Sweep  Foraging Fortune  Hunting Fortune ☯ Hunting Wisdom; Majestic Bonus Grants +50 Speed while in the  Critter Safari.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Morning Dew is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Majestic]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to the [[Safari Belt]]. It grants [[Hunting Fortune]], [[Foraging Fortune]], [[Sweep]] and [[Hunting Wisdom]].
## Obtaining
Morning Dew can be purchased from the [[Essence Shops|Safari Essence Shop]] for 2,500 (39 stacks plus 4) [[Safari Essence]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Morning Dew can be combined with a [[Safari Belt]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Majestic]] reforge, to grant [[Hunting Fortune]], [[Foraging Fortune]], [[Sweep]] and [[Hunting Wisdom]], which scale with the Belt's rarity. This has no [[Mining]] requirement.
Additionally, it grants the Majestic Bonus, which grants the player 50 [[Speed]] while in the [[Critter Safari]].

# Mouse Trap
Categories: Epic items, Items, Pages with UIs, Tools
[Mouse Trap]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Pest Trap]]
Next →: [[Vermin Trap]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MOUSE_PEST_TRAP
Tooltip: Place in a Garden plot and fill with bait to catch  Pests while offline.; Maximum Capacity: 3 Pests Limit of 3 Traps; Field Mice are 3x more likely to be caught in this type of trap!; EPIC
A Mouse Trap is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
Mouse Traps can be purchased from [[Pesthunter Phillip]] for 75 [[Pests (Currency)|Pests]] and a [[Pest Trap]].
## Usage
Mouse Traps can be placed in [[The Garden|Garden Plots]] to occasionally catch [[Pests]]. Up to 3 Mouse Traps can be placed at once. Pest baits can be placed inside to increase the chance of certain [[Pests]] spawning. Every [[Pests|Pest]] that spawns in a trap consumes one Pest Bait.
- [[Compost]] attracts [[Mosquito|Mosquitos]] and [[Earthworm|Earthworms]].
- [[Plant Matter]] attracts [[Locust|Locusts]] and [[Slug|Slugs]].
- [[Dung]] attracts [[Beetle|Beetles]] and [[Fly|Flies]].
- [[Honey Jar|Honey Jars]] attract [[Moth|Moths]] and [[Cricket|Crickets]].
- [[Tasty Cheese]] attracts [[Rat|Rats]] and [[Mite|Mites]].
- [[Jelly]] attracts [[Dragonfly|Dragonflies]] (only during the day), [[Firefly|Fireflies]] (only during the night), and [[Praying Mantis|Praying Mantises]].
- [[Moondew]] guarantees catching [[Lunar Moth|Lunar Moths]].
Additionally, [[Field Mouse|Field Mice]] are 3x more likely to be caught.

The Mr Claws Rat Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on November 18, 2023. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 1,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Mr Claws Rat Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Rat Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

The Murderwing Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Sky High]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Murderwing Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: Phantom Family
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Murderwing Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Sky High]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Sky High ([[Attributes]]); Increases the distance you can fall before taking fall damage by +0.5–5 blocks.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Murderwing Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Murderwing Shard has the following description:
- Murderwings reside high above the clouds. Their vision is so good that, even on the cloudiest of nights, they can spot prey on the forest floor far below.

Tooltip: Defense: +250; While worn, gain +1  Speed for every 20 pieces of Obsidian in your inventory!; Bonus Speed: 0; Immune to Wither effect.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 14.; EPIC CHESTPLATE
Tutorial:Avoiding scams
Warning! This item is known to be frequently substituted with [[Shadow Assassin Armor|Shadow Assassin Chestplate]] in scams. Please refer to Tutorial:Avoiding scams for further information.
The Obsidian Chestplate is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Chestplates|Chestplate]] dropped by [[Obsidian Defender|Obsidian Defenders]]. It grants the player [[Speed]] based on the amount of [[Obsidian]] in their inventory.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv44]|[[Obsidian Defender]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
## Usage
Chestplate:
Obsidian Chestplate [[Defense]]: +250
[Effects]
[While worn, gain +1 Speed for every 20 pieces of Obsidian in your inventory!; Immune to Wither effect.]
The item effect does not count obsidian located in the player's [[Storage]] or [[Mining Sack]]. It can also be [[Salvaging|salvaged]] for 3 [[Dragon Essence]]
## Trivia
- The maximum additional [[Speed]] that can be given to a player through the Obsidian Chestplate's ability is +112 [[Speed]] (with 35 x 64 = 2,240 (35 stacks) Obsidian or 2,240 Enchanted Obsidian in their inventory.)
- The Obsidian Chestplate is one of 9 armor pieces which have the darkest color with a hex color code of ⁠ #000000. The others are [[Bat Person Armor]], [[Squid Boots]], [[Tarantula Armor]], [[Shadow Assassin Armor]], [[Rancher's Boots]], [[Necromancer Lord Armor|Necromancer Lord Chestplate]], [[Snorkeling Armor]] and [[Wither Armor]].

Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell (Motes): 200 [[Motes]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: OBSOLITE
Tooltip: Collection Item; Practically a fossil.; Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
Obsolite is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] collection item. As an ore, it has the appearance of purple stained glass pane.
## Collection
Collecting [[Youngite]], [[Timite]], or Obsolite increases the player's [[Timite]] [[Collections|collection]], unlocking useful items and rewarding [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] at specific milestones.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|25|[[Time Gun]] Recipe; [[Eon Pickaxe]] Recipe|+4|
|II|50|Time Pocket Bag ([[Time Pocket]]) Upgrade|+4|
|III|100|[[Chrono Pickaxe]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|250|[[Highlite]] Recipe|+4|
|V|500|[[Satelite]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|750|[[Discrite]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
Obsolite can be obtained by using a [[Time Gun]] on [[Timite]] for 2 seconds until it turns purple, then breaking it with a [[Chrono Pickaxe]]. Only 1 Obsolite will drop.
Attempting to use a [[Time Gun]] on Obsolite results in the following message:
PARADOX! This ore has maximum time wackification!
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Discrite]]|Timite IV (4) ([[Timite]])|64x [[Youngite]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Timite]] [[Obsolite]] 256x Obsolite|Recipe: Obsolite x64 ×4 + [[Timite]] x64 ×4 + [[Youngite]] x64 → [[Discrite]]|
|[[Highlite]]|Timite IV (4) ([[Timite]])|[[Obsolite]] 16x Obsolite 32x [[Timite]] 32x [[Youngite]]|Recipe: [[Youngite]] x16 ×2 + [[Timite]] x16 ×2 + Obsolite x16 → [[Highlite]]|
|[[Satelite]]|Timite V (5) ([[Timite]])|1x [[Highlite]] [[Obsolite]] 32x Obsolite 32x [[Timite]] 64x [[Youngite]]|Recipe: [[Youngite]] x16 ×4 + [[Timite]] x16 ×2 + Obsolite x16 ×2 + [[Highlite]] → [[Satelite]]|
### NPC Trading
[[Obsolite]] 20 Obsolite can be used to purchase [[Shen's Ringalia]] from [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X]].
|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|
|-|-|-|
|[[Shen's Ringalia]]|Completion of Eleanor's Puzzle|25,000 [[Motes]] 64x [[Puff Crux]] 20x [[Youngite]] 20x [[Timite]] [[Obsolite]] 20x Obsolite 1x [[Disinfestor Gloves]]|

An Old Leather Boot is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item obtained from [[Fishing]] while using a [[Junk Sinker]].
## Obtaining
An Old Leather Boot can be obtained as an OUTSTANDING JUNK CATCH by fishing with a [[Junk Sinker]] applied to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]].
## Usage

Old Leather Boots can be used to purchase various items from [[Junker Joel]].

|[[Drone Mod: Contraband]]|None|[[Old Leather Boot]] 1x Old Leather Boot 1x [[Water Orb]] 8x [[Busted Belt Buckle]] 64x [[Rusty Coin]]|Recipe: Old Leather Boot + [[Rusty Coin]] x32 ×2 + [[Water Orb]] + [[Busted Belt Buckle]] x8 → [[Drone Mod: Contraband]]|

Categories: Epic items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Wolf Slayer, Mob Drops

Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: OVERFLUX_CAPACITOR
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
The Overflux Capacitor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item. It is a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drop from the Tier IV [[Sven Packmaster]]. The player must have at least [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer VII (7)]]. It is used in the crafting recipe for the [[Overflux Power Orb]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
An Overflux Capacitor can drop from the Tier IV [[Sven Packmaster]] if the player has reached [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer LVL VII (7)]]. It has a 0.0385% (approximately 1/2,597) base chance to drop from the boss.
### Raffle of the Century
Overflux Capacitor can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Overflux Power Orb]]|Wolf Slayer LVL VII (7) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|[[Overflux Capacitor]] 1x Overflux Capacitor 1x [[Mana Flux Power Orb]] 24x [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Golden Tooth]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] x32 ×4 + Overflux Capacitor + [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] x8 ×3 + [[Mana Flux Power Orb]] → [[Overflux Power Orb]]|
## Trivia
- This Overflux Capacitor's name is a reference to the Flux Capacitor in Back To The Future.
- This is considered to be the best value drop from Sven Packmasters.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Added Overflux Capacitor.|

Tooltip: Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK Place an orb for 1m buffing up to 5; players within 18 blocks.; Mana Cost: 50% of max Vitality Cost: 50; Orb Buff: Overflux • Heal yourself for 50❤ per second.; • Heal others for 25❤ per second.; • Gain +100 Health Regen.; • Grants +100% base mana regen.; • Grants +25 Strength.; Only one deployable buff applies.; ☠ Requires Wolf Slayer 7.; EPIC DEPLOYABLE
The Overflux Power Orb is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Power Orbs|Power Orb]] unlocked at [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer VII (7)]]. It is an upgraded form of the [[Mana Flux Power Orb]], and it can be upgraded to a [[Plasmaflux Power Orb]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wolf Slayer LVL VII (7) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x [[Overflux Capacitor]] 1x [[Mana Flux Power Orb]] 24x [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Golden Tooth]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] x32 ×4 + [[Overflux Capacitor]] + [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] x8 ×3 + [[Mana Flux Power Orb]] → Overflux Power Orb|
## Usage
Deployable:
Overflux Power Orb
[Effects]
[Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK; Place an orb for 1m buffing up to 5 players within 18 blocks.; Mana Cost: 50% of max; Vitality Cost: 50 Vitality; Orb Buff: Overflux; Heal yourself for 50 Health per second.; Heal others for 25 Health per second.; Gain +100 Health Regen.; Grants +100% base mana regen.; Grants +25 Strength.; Only one deployable buff applies.]
Using its ability does not remove or use up the item. When placed, it cannot be picked up; the ability spawns a new orb.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Plasmaflux Power Orb]]|Wolf Slayer LVL VIII (8) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|[[Overflux Power Orb]] 1x Overflux Power Orb 1x [[Plasma Nucleus]] 72x (1 stack plus 8 (72 in total)) [[Golden Tooth]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Enchanted Obsidian]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Obsidian]] x64 ×4 + [[Plasma Nucleus]] + [[Golden Tooth]] x24 ×3 + Overflux Power Orb → [[Plasmaflux Power Orb]]|

- The texture of the [[Blood Key]] has the same texture as an Overflux Power Orb.

# Overgrown Grass
Aliases: Grass (disambiguation), Mossy, Mossy Reforge, Mossy reforge
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: OVERGROWN_GRASS
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Mossy reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Farming Fortune  Speed; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 23.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Overgrown Grass is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Mossy]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]]. It grants [[Speed]] and [[Farming Fortune]].
## Obtaining
### Garden Visitors
Overgrown Grass has a 0.1% (1 in 1,000) chance to be offered by [[Garden Visitors]] in [[The Garden]].
### Harvest Bounties
Overgrown Grass has a 0.1% (1 in 1,000) chance to be obtained from a [[Greenhouse|Harvest Bounty]] in the [[Greenhouse]].
### DNA Analysis Milestone
One Overgrown Grass is rewarded at [[Crop Analyzer|DNA Analysis Milestone II]], which requires analyzing 10 [[Mutations]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Overgrown Grass can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Mossy]] reforge, to grant [[Speed]] and [[Farming Fortune]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIII (23)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Mossy]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+3 Spd ([[Speed]]) +5 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+3 Spd ([[Speed]]) +10 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+5 Spd ([[Speed]]) +15 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+5 Spd ([[Speed]]) +20 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+7 Spd ([[Speed]]) +25 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+7 Spd ([[Speed]]) +30 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+10 Spd ([[Speed]]) +35 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|

An Overpriced Drink is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Overpriced]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]]. It grants [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Overbloom]].

### Reforging
Overpriced Drink can be combined with a [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Overpriced]] reforge, to grant [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Overbloom]], which scale with the Farming Tool's rarity. This has no [[Mining]] requirement.

The Pandarai Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Foraging Wisdom]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Pandarai Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: Panda Family
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Pandarai Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Foraging Wisdom]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Foraging Wisdom ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Foraging Wisdom]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

The Pernimius Glyph is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Quest Item obtained in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
The Pernimius Glyph can be obtained after starting [[Sorcerer Okron]]'s quest and imbueing the [[Fortis Glyph]], then opening the chest at [[Coordinates|-77.5, 71, -148.5]] by the water tower within the [[Dreadfarm]]. Once given to Okron, it can no longer be obtained.
## Usage
The Pernimius Glyph can be given to [[Sorcerer Okron]]. When given, he will task the player with using the [[Warding Diamathame]] to imbue a spell onto the house.

Upgradeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 202,400x (3,162 stacks plus 32 (202,400 in total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]]) 36,960x (577 stacks plus 32 (36,960 in total)) [[Cobblestone]]
Raw Materials to upgrade: 179,200x (2,800 stacks (179,200 items total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PERSONAL_COMPACTOR_6000
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +12; Automatically turns certain materials in your inventory into their enchanted form.; Right-click to configure!; Does not grant Carpentry XP!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
Personal Compactor 6000 is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Redstone Dust|Redstone Dust VIII (8)]]. Its ability turns certain materials in the inventory into their enchanted form similar to the [[Super Compactor 3000]]. This saves time crafting when grinding large quantities of items.
The auto-compaction abilities of different tiers of Personal Compactors work together with separate compacted items.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Redstone Dust VIII (8) ([[Redstone Dust]])|1x [[Personal Compactor 5000]] 7x [[Enchanted Redstone Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] ×7 + [[Personal Compactor 5000]] → Personal Compactor 6000|
## Usage
Right-clicking the accessory while in the hand will open its configuration menu, where the player can place up to seven different Enchanted Items in Auto-Crafting slots. When there are enough items in the player's inventory, it will automatically turn them into the Enchanted form. It has 7 slots.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Personal Compactor 7000]]|Redstone Dust IX (9) ([[Redstone Dust]])|[[Personal Compactor 6000]] 1x Personal Compactor 6000 21x [[Enchanted Redstone Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] x3 ×7 + Personal Compactor 6000 → [[Personal Compactor 7000]]|
## Trivia
- [[Experience Artifact]], Personal Compactor, [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]], [[Book of Progression]], [[Melody's Hair]], [[Zombie Talisman]], [[Skeleton Talisman]], [[Archaeologist's Compass]], [[Lucky Hoof|Lucky]], [[Eternal Hoof]], [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]] and [[Ring of Love|Yellow Rock of Love]] are the only accessories to use a vanilla sprite, rather than a custom made one.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 7, 2020|0.7.10|Added Personal Compactor 6000.|
|Jun 7, 2020|0.7.10|Updated recipe to require a [[Personal Compactor 5000]] instead of a [[Super Compactor 3000]].|
|Jun 10, 2020|Now requires 28 Enchanted Redstone Blocks ([[Enchanted Redstone Block]]) to craft, from 56.|
|Unknown Date|No longer gives [[Carpentry]] xp.|
|Sep 21, 2021|Items placed in the Personal Compactor 6000 are no longer placed inside, but a duplicate, unusable item is placed inside.|
|Aug 25, 2023|Fixed adding the same items to Personal Compactor/Deletor.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed Crafting Recipe from 28 Enchanted Redstone Blocks ([[Enchanted Redstone Block]]) and 1 [[Personal Compactor 5000]] to 7 Enchanted Redstone Blocks ([[Enchanted Redstone Block]]) and 1 [[Personal Compactor 5000]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed crafting requirement from Redstone Dust XIII (13) ([[Redstone Dust]]) to Redstone Dust VIII (8) ([[Redstone Dust]]).|

# Personal Deletor 6000
Categories: Epic items, Accessories, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Iron Ingot Collection
[Personal Deletor 6000]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Iron Ingot XI (11) ([[Iron Ingot]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Personal Deletor 5000]]
Next →: [[Personal Deletor 7000]]
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 125,920x (1,967 stacks plus 32 (125,920 in total)) [[Iron Ingot]] 51,200x (800 stacks (51,200 items total)) [[Coal]] 25,600x (400 stacks (25,600 items total)) [[Redstone Dust]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PERSONAL_DELETOR_6000
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +12; Automatically delete specific items on pickup.; Right-click to configure!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
The Personal Deletor 6000 is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingot XI (11)]]. Its ability automatically deletes certain materials in one's inventory when picked up. It is the upgrade of the [[Personal Deletor 5000]], and can upgrade to the [[Personal Deletor 7000]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Iron Ingot XI (11) ([[Iron Ingot]])|1x [[Personal Deletor 5000]] 448x (7 stacks (448 items total)) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x64 ×7 + [[Personal Deletor 5000]] → Personal Deletor 6000|
## Usage
Right-clicking the accessory while in the hand will open its configuration menu, where the player can place three items in the Auto-Deleting slot, similar to the [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactors]]. When the respective item enters the player's inventory, it will get deleted. It has 7 slots and also has a switch that must be turned on in order for the device to function.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Personal Deletor 7000]]|Iron Ingot XII (12) ([[Iron Ingot]])|[[Personal Deletor 6000]] 1x Personal Deletor 6000 7x [[Enchanted Iron Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×7 + Personal Deletor 6000 → [[Personal Deletor 7000]]|
## Trivia
- The [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]] will only remove items from your inventory that you pick up off the ground. This is regardless of the [[Rarity]] of the item. However, it will not delete any item that does not have the same SkyBlock ID of the item in the deletor.
  - Example: You obtain a personal deletor and place a [[Golden Sword]] inside, any item you pick up with the SkyBlock ID of GOLDEN_SWORD will be deleted. The [[Midas' Sword]], which uses the Golden Sword item for its texture, will not be deleted upon pickup, as it does not have the SkyBlock ID of GOLDEN_SWORD.
- Unlike mentioned previously, when the Personal Deletors were first released, if players placed a vanilla iron sword in the GUI, it would also cause every in-game item with an iron sword texture (e.g. the [[Giant's Sword]] and [[Hyperion]]) to be deleted. This led to the Deletor being temporarily disabled.
  - However, certain items that share the same SkyBlock ID will still get deleted. For example, since all potions have a SkyBlock ID of POTION, putting any potion in a personal deletor would result in all potions being deleted.
    - This was partially patched in the 0.19.10 update by preventing potions from being placed in Personal Deletors.
- Secrets could be deleted without receiving credit for finding them, by putting a Secret item like a [[Decoy]] in a Personal Deletor and attempting to pick up the item.
  - This was later patched.
- [[Experience Artifact]], [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactor]], Personal Deletor, [[Book of Progression]], [[Melody's Hair]], [[Zombie Talisman]], [[Skeleton Talisman]], [[Archaeologist's Compass]], [[Lucky Hoof]], [[Eternal Hoof]], [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]] and [[Ring of Love|Yellow Rock of Love]] are the only accessories to use a vanilla sprite, rather than a custom made one.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Mar 9, 2021|0.11.2|Added Personal Deletor 6000.|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Fixed Personal Deletor not working on loots from Frozen Treasures ([[Glacial Cave]]).|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed the Personal Deletor not deleting double catch items from [[Fishing]].|
|Aug 25, 2023|Fixed adding the same items to Personal Compactor/Deletor.|
|Jan 16, 2024|0.19.10|Potions can no longer be placed in Personal Deletors.|

A placed personal harp
The Personal Harp is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] piece of [[Furniture]] that allows the player to play all the songs that you can on [[Melody|Melody's harp]] on the plateau plus two extra prodigy songs, "Through the Campfire", and "Pachelbel".
## Obtaining
The Personal Harp can be purchased from [[Melody]] for 600,000 [[Coins]], but only after one has obtained 90% or higher on "Greensleeves" (the 6th song), and all the songs before it.
## Exclusive Songs
The Personal Harp has two exclusive songs after the main songs, "Through the Campfire" and "Pachelbel".
Clearing the song with 90% accuracy will reward +1 [[Intelligence]]. Clearing "Pachelbel" will reward +1 [[Intelligence]], and a [[Tuning Fork]]. Every time you complete "Pachelbel" with a perfect score, you will get another Tuning Fork.

|Through the Campfire|Prodigy|+1 [[Intelligence]]; +35 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|𝄕𝄞 2 3 4 2 3 4 5 3 𝄀 6 4 5 3 4 5 4 3 𝄀 2 3 4 2 3 4 5 3 𝄀 6 4 5 3 4 5 4 3 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 2 3 4 2 3 4 5 3 𝄀 6 4 5 3 4 5 4 3 𝄀 2 3 4 2 3 4 5 3 𝄀 6 4 5 3 4 5 4 3 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 2 3 4 2 3 4 5 3 𝄀 6 4 5 3 4 5 4 3 𝄀 2 3 4 2 3 4 5 3 𝄀 6 4 5 3 4 5 7 6 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 5 6 5 4 5 4 3 4 𝄀 3 2 3 2 1 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 3 • 1 3 • 1 3 1 𝄀 3 3 1 3 • 1 3 1 𝄀 6 • 3 6 • 3 6 3 𝄀 6 6 3 6 • 3 6 3 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 5 • 2 5 • 2 5 2 𝄀 5 5 2 5 • 2 𝄀 4 3 2 3 2 1 • 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 3 • 1 3 • 1 3 1 𝄀 3 3 1 3 • 1 3 1 𝄀 6 • 3 6 • 3 6 3 𝄀 6 7 3 6 • 3 6 3 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 5 • 2 5 • 2 5 2 𝄀 5 5 2 5 • 2 𝄀 4 3 2 3 2 1 • 𝄀 2 7 • • • • 𝄂|
|Pachelbel|Prodigy|+1 [[Intelligence]]; +35 SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]); [[Tuning Fork]]|𝄕𝄞 6 • • • 4 • • • 𝄀 5 • • • 2 • • • 𝄀 3 • • • 1 • • • 𝄀 3 • • • 4 • • • 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 4 • 3 • 4 • 1 • 𝄀 1 • • • 3 • • 𝄀 4 • • 5 • • 𝄀 6 • • 6 • 7 • 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 6 • 5 • 4 • 7 • 𝄀 6 • 5 • 4 • 3 • 𝄀 2 • 1 • 3 • 3 • 𝄀 4 3 2 • • 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 6 • 4 5 6 • 4 5 𝄀 6 1 2 3 4 5 6 7 𝄀 6 • 2 3 4 • 2 3 𝄀 4 5 4 3 4 6 5 6 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 3 • 6 5 4 • 3 2 𝄀 3 2 1 2 3 4 5 6 𝄀 4 • 6 5 6 • 3 2 𝄀 3 4 5 6 7 6 • 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 4 5 6 • 5 4 𝄀 5 3 4 5 6 5 4 3 𝄀 4 • 2 3 4 • 4 5 𝄀 6 7 6 5 6 • 5 4 5 𝄀; 𝄕𝄞 3 • 5 4 3 • 3 2 𝄀 3 2 1 2 3 4 5 6 𝄀 4 • 6 5 6 • 3 2 𝄀 3 4 5 6 7 4 𝄂|
## Trivia
- Visitors on an island that has a personal harp cannot use it. However, personal harps can be traded to people who have not yet unlocked the ability to buy one themselves.
- There is a disadvantage of using Personal Harp. On the private island, although there are fewer lag spikes, they are still common. Lags last longer than those on public islands.
  - Going to another island for 30 seconds to unload the private island and going back will usually bring the player to an internal server with less players, and thus less lag.
- The benefit of playing the songs on a Personal Harp is that you don't have to wait for other people to finish their songs, as you're the only one who can use it.
- "Through the Campfire" is an edit of the intro to "Through the Fire and Flames" by Dragonforce, infamously known as the hardest song in Activision's Guitar Hero III: Legends of Rock.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 18, 2019|Added Personal Harp.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PETRIFIED_STARFALL
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Stellar reforge when combined with a Mining Tool.; It's not actually a star.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Speed  Mining Fortune; Stellar Bonus Earn +20% ᠅ Mithril Powder and +75 Mining Fortune when mining near  Fallen Stars.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 26.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Petrified Starfall is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Stellar]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] or the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Fortune]].
## Obtaining
The Petrified Starfall can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]]. It requires 512 (8 stacks) [[Starfall]] and 6 hours to forge.
## Usage
### Reforging
Petrified Starfall can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Stellar]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Mining Speed]], which scale with the Mining Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXVI (26)]]. A Mining Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Stellar]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Stellar Bonus, which grants +20% ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] and 75 [[Mining Fortune]] when mining near [[Fallen Stars]].

# Pickonimbus 2000
Aliases: Pickonimbus
Categories: Epic items, Items, Crystal Hollows, Tools, Mining
[Pickonimbus 2000]
Type: Tool
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
HotM ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) Requirement: Heart of the Mountain IV (4) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])
Stats: [[Damage]]: 30; [[Breaking Power]]: 8; [[Mining Speed]]: 750
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 12 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PICKONIMBUS
Tooltip: Breaking Power 8; Damage: +30; Mining Speed: +750; A powerful tool that breaks after 2,000 uses.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 4.; EPIC PICKAXE
The Pickonimbus 2000 is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] obtained in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. It breaks after 2,000 uses.
## Obtaining
A Pickonimbus 2000 has a 37.48% to 48.89% chance to appear in a [[Crystal Nucleus]] loot bundle. It also has a 0.14% chance to appear in a [[Crystal Hollows|Treasure Chest]]. There is a 0.07% chance for it to be found when using a [[Metal Detector]] in the [[Mines of Divan]] when having found all [[Scavenged Items]] and a 0.06% chance when not having found all [[Scavenged Items]].
## Usage
Pickaxe:
Pickonimbus 2000 [[Damage]]: +30 [[Breaking Power]]: +8 [[Mining Speed]]: +750
[Effects]
[A powerful tool that breaks after 2000 uses.]
The Pickonimbus 2000 requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]] to use.
### Salvaging
Pickonimbus 2000 can be salvaged for 5 [[Diamond Essence]].
## Trivia
- The Pickonimbus 2000 is a reference to the Nimbus 2000 broomstick from the Harry Potter franchise.
- This is the only pickaxe to have durability.
- The pickaxe does not have a durability bar even though it breaks after 2,000 uses. Instead, the number of uses left is displayed in the lore.
- A Pickonimbus 2000 can be repaired to full durability in an [[Anvil]] by placing a brand new unenchanted Pickonimbus in the first slot and the damaged one in the second slot. It will transfer over enchantments, but not reforges. However, it resets the [[Compact]] counter (but not the enchantment's level), making [[Compact|4]] the highest level attainable on a Pickonimbus 2000 that didn't already have a higher level before SkyBlock 0.19.1.
- Unlike [[Drills|Drill]] Fuel, blocks mined by [[Mining Spread]] and abilities that break multiple blocks at once (such as Pickobulus) also remove durability from the Pickonimbus 2000.
- It is not possible to auction a Pickonimbus 2000 below 2,000 durability, but they can still be traded.
- In the SkyBlock 0.20.6 update, existing Pickonimbus 2000s that were at full durability were reduced to 2,000 durability, but ones that were between 2,001 and 4,999 durability kept their extra durability.
  - This is because Pickonimbuses at full durability do not have a pickonimbus_durability tag in their NBT data, and damaged ones were not modified.
  - This update also resulted in Pickonimbuses with more than 2,000 but less than 5,000 durability becoming auctionable.

Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PLANTBOY_ADVANCE
Tooltip: This plant loves a good challenge.; Every time you harvest it, it'll challenge you to a quick game. Win, and it thrives. Lose, though, and it's game over for this little guy.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; EPIC MUTATION
A PlantBoy Advance is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A PlantBoy Advance can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 6 [[Snoozling]] and 6 [[Thunderling]] within 1 block of a 2x2 area of farmland.
While growing, a PlantBoy Advance does not need water and has 12 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it increases yield by 20%.
In order to harvest a PlantBoy Advance, the player needs to collect 8 apples in a game of Snake. If the player fails the minigame they may repeat it again immediately with no penalty.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, PlantBoy Advance also drops 4,600 (71 stacks plus 56) [[Moonflower]], 4,600 [[Sunflower]], 4,600 [[Wheat]], and +36,800 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A PlantBoy Advance can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 700,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 350 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a PlantBoy Advance grants an additional 1,250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
PlantBoy Advances are used to create an [[All-in Aloe]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]|None|[[PlantBoy Advance]] 1x PlantBoy Advance 2x [[Chirping Stereo]] 3x [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 32x [[Enchanted Paper]] 64x [[Botroot]]|Recipe: [[Chirping Stereo]] ×2 + PlantBoy Advance + [[Botroot]] x32 ×2 + [[Enchanted Paper]] x32 + [[Enchanted Iron Block]] ×3 → [[Super Jacob Entertainment System]]|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Attempting to harvest|[CROP] PlantBoy Advance: I doubt you are a real gamer. I challenge you to a game of Snake! [ACCEPT]|
|Successfully collecting 8 apples|[CROP] PlantBoy Advance: You bested me, you may harvest me now.|
## Trivia
- The minigame is almost identical to Snake on the [[Abiphones]], save for the 8 apple target.
- The PlantBoy Advance is a reference to the Game Boy Advance.
## Gallery
- PlantBoy Advance in the Greenhouse PlantBoy Advance in the [[Greenhouse]]

|Sep 30, 2026|Failing the minigame no longer reduces the Growth Stages by 3.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added PlantBoy Advance.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Failing the minigame no longer reduces the Growth Stages by 3.|

# Potato Spreading
Categories: Epic items, Minion upgrades, Minion Modifiers, The Uprising
[Potato Spreading]
Type: Minion Upgrade ([[Minion Upgrades]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Function: The minion occasionally collects a [[Potato]], alongside the regular item it was collecting.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Sell: 250,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: POTATO_SPREADING
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade. Place it in ONE minion and it will occasionally generate Potatoes!; EPIC
Potato Spreading is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]] similar to [[Diamond Spreading]]. Any [[Minions]] with this upgrade will occasionally collect a [[Potato]], alongside the regular item it was collecting.
## Obtaining
A Potato Spreading has a 1.45% (1 in 69.03 pigs) chance to be dropped by [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] during the [[Year of the Pig]].
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
Potato Spreading can be placed in all [[Minions]]. It is limited to one minion per profile at a time. For every item that the minion harvests, it has a 0.05 (1 in 20) chance to generate a [[Potato]]. Minions that collect multiple items at once, such as [[Snow Minion|Snow Minions]], can make multiple potatoes at once. Potato Spreading does not affect the minion's speed or other resources generated by the minion.
This can also be used to provide a small amount of additional [[Farming]] experience from the minion.
## Trivia
- This item was added as part of 7YouTuber [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]]'s mayor perks, due to his position as a [[Resistance Leader]].
## Bugs
- When first released, the Potato Spreading would generate a bugged "Potatoes" item with a pluralized name. This does not count as a normal [[Potato]].
  - They do not give farming EXP while collecting, stack with normal potatoes, fit into [[Sacks]], cannot be sold to [[Bazaar]], nor can be crafted into an enchanted item.
- When first released, the Potato Spreading was able to be put on each minion by replacing a different minion upgrade.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 16, 2021|Added Potato Spreading.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Potato Spreading to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Revamped the [[Shiny Pig]] loot table.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Added NPC sell price of 250,000 [[Coins]].|

The Potentia Glyph is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Quest Item obtained in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
The Potentia Glyph can be obtained after starting [[Sorcerer Okron]]'s quest and imbuing the [[Robur Glyph]], then opening the chest in a server room at [[Coordinates|-57.5, 80, -14.5]] within the [[West Village]]. Once given to Okron, it can no longer be obtained.
## Usage
The Potentia Glyph can be given to [[Sorcerer Okron]]. When given, he will task the player with using the [[Warding Diamathame]] to imbue a spell onto the house.

The Power Crystal is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that can be obtained from [[The Forge|Forging]]. They are used to power [[Beacon|Beacons]] for 48 hours. Multiple can be placed in a [[Beacon]] at once.
## Obtaining
The Power Crystal can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It requires 256 (4 stacks) [[Starfall|Starfalls]] and 2 hours to forge.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: AMBER_POWER_SCROLL
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable: ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum: ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: OPAL_POWER_SCROLL
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Gain +10 Health Regen for 5s when using your RIGHT CLICK Item Ability. Has a 5s cooldown.; Can be combined with any item with a RIGHT CLICK Item Ability at an anvil.; EPIC
Power Scrolls are [[Rarity|EPIC]] items that can be applied to any item with a RIGHT CLICK ability. They are obtained via crafting 48 [[Enchanted Paper]] and 16 Fine [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] of the Scroll type. Recipes for all Power Scroll variants are unlocked at [[Gemstone|Gemstone VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ruby Power Scroll ([[Power Scrolls]])|Gemstone VII (7) ([[Gemstone]])|16x Fine Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 48x [[Enchanted Paper]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Paper]] x16 ×3 + [[Gemstone]] x16 → [[Power Scrolls]]|
|Sapphire Power Scroll ([[Power Scrolls]])|Gemstone VII (7) ([[Gemstone]])|16x Fine Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 48x [[Enchanted Paper]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Paper]] x16 ×3 + [[Gemstone]] x16 → [[Power Scrolls]]|
|Jasper Power Scroll ([[Power Scrolls]])|Gemstone VII (7) ([[Gemstone]])|16x Fine Jasper Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 48x [[Enchanted Paper]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Paper]] x16 ×3 + [[Gemstone]] x16 → [[Power Scrolls]]|
|Amethyst Power Scroll ([[Power Scrolls]])|Gemstone VII (7) ([[Gemstone]])|16x Fine Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 48x [[Enchanted Paper]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Paper]] x16 ×3 + [[Gemstone]] x16 → [[Power Scrolls]]|
|Amber Power Scroll ([[Power Scrolls]])|Gemstone VII (7) ([[Gemstone]])|16x Fine Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 48x [[Enchanted Paper]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Paper]] x16 ×3 + [[Gemstone]] x16 → [[Power Scrolls]]|
|Opal Power Scroll ([[Power Scrolls]])|Gemstone VII (7) ([[Gemstone]])|16x Fine Opal Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 48x [[Enchanted Paper]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Paper]] x16 ×3 + [[Gemstone]] x16 → [[Power Scrolls]]|
## Usage
Combining an item with a Power Scroll in an [[Anvil]] will add the Power Scroll's effect to the item. Only items with RIGHT CLICK abilities can have Power Scrolls applied to them: activating an ability will trigger the Power Scroll.
Power Scrolls do not stack.
### Effects
These effects are applied when using an item's RIGHT CLICK ability.
|Scroll|Effect|
|-|-|
|Ruby|Gain +10 [[Health Regen]] for 5s|
|Sapphire|+5 [[Mana]] (5s cooldown)|
|Jasper|+10 [[Strength]] for 5s|
|Amethyst|+10 [[Defense]] for 5s|
|Amber|+30 [[Mining Speed]] for 5s|
|Opal|+10 [[True Defense]] for 5s|
## Tips
- A [[Power Scrolls|Ruby Power Scroll]] can be used on [[Zombie Sword]], [[Ornate Zombie Sword]], [[Florid Zombie Sword]] and/or [[Wands|Healing Wands]] since these items' right ability heals players.
- [[Power Scrolls|Sapphire Power Scroll]] can be used on Mage Weapons such as the [[Spirit Sceptre]] and [[Bonzo's Staff]]. It can also be used on [[Soul Whip]] for [[Enderman Slayer]] to regenerate Mana by casting it to the [[Voidgloom Seraph]]. It can also buff [[Aspect of the End]]/ [[Aspect of the Void]] when teleporting.
- [[Power Scrolls|Jasper Power Scroll]] can be used on a weapon that has high Damage such as an [[One For All]] weapon that has an ability and/or a [[Giant's Sword]].
- [[Power Scrolls|Amethyst Power Scroll]] can be used on items that give [[Defense]] such as the [[Wither Cloak Sword]].
- [[Power Scrolls|Amber Power Scroll]] can be used with any [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] or [[Drills|Drill]] due to them all sharing an ability from the [[Heart of the Mountain]] tree.
  - The [[Mining Speed]] from the [[Power Scrolls|Amber Power Scroll]] is not accounted for in Mining Speed Boost if activated alongside it: the player will only gain the usual 30 . However, it is if the [[Power Scrolls|Amber Power Scroll]] was already active before activating Mining Speed Boost: the player will gain the base 30 as well as the +x% . This can be done by applying it on another item with a Right-Click ability such as the [[Aspect of the Void]].
- [[Power Scrolls|Opal Power Scroll]] can be used on Blaze Slayer daggers( [[Firedust Dagger]], [[Twilight Dagger]], [[Kindlebane Dagger]], [[Mawdredge Dagger]], [[Pyrochaos Dagger]] and [[Deathripper Dagger]]) since [[Inferno Demonlord]] does entirely [[True Damage]], and swapping [[Inferno Demonlord|Attunements]] is essential during the boss fight.
## Trivia
- Items that have a Power Scroll on them will display "⦾" in the color of the Scroll, in front of Ability: ⦾ for Ruby, ⦾ For Sapphire, ⦾ for Jasper, ⦾ for Amethyst, ⦾ for Amber, and ⦾ for Opal.
- There is no Power Scroll variants for [[Gemstone|Jade]], [[Gemstone|Topaz]], [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Onyx (disambiguation)|Onyx]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]] or [[Gemstone|Peridot]].
  - However, [[Gemstone|Jade]] and [[Gemstone|Topaz]] have been found in the SkyBlock items API, meaning that they may be added to the game.
- Any item with a right-click ability but without RIGHT CLICK in its description cannot have a Power Scroll applied to it, such as [[Power Orbs]].
- The item name color does not match the color of the rarity, but the color of the gemstones used in crafting it.
- Using abilities of multiple items with the same scroll does not stack the buffs.

# Pre-Digestion Fish
Aliases: Pre-digestion Fish
Categories: Epic items, Items, Pages with non-auctionable items with valid auctionable item IDs, Crafting ingredients

Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PRE_DIGESTION_FISH
Tooltip: The obvious result of mixing cat food, spacetime bending and mystical mythology.; Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
Pre-Digestion Fish is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item obtained by feeding [[Tasty Cat Food]] to [[Siamese Lynxes]]. It is used to craft [[Four-Eyed Fish]] and the [[Future Calories Talisman]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Future Calories Talisman]]|None|[[Pre-Digestion Fish]] 1x Pre-Digestion Fish 48x [[Silver Magmafish]] 384x (6 stacks (384 items total)) [[Enchanted Raw Cod]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Raw Cod]] x64 ×6 + [[Silver Magmafish]] x24 ×2 + Pre-Digestion Fish → [[Future Calories Talisman]]|
|[[Four-Eyed Fish]]|None|1x [[Lucky Clover]] [[Pre-Digestion Fish]] 1x Pre-Digestion Fish 1x [[Reinforced Scales]] 384x [[Enchanted Spider Eye]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] x64 ×6 + [[Lucky Clover]] + Pre-Digestion Fish + [[Reinforced Scales]] → [[Four-Eyed Fish]]|

Tooltip: Can be given to Professor Robot to complete the Sapphire Crystal Quest.; This decaying piece of machinery was once a part of a large robot known as Automaton Prime. There's still a lot of good stuff in here.; EPIC
A Precursor Apparatus is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that can be given to [[Professor Robot]] to complete the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]] quest in the [[Lost Precursor City]].
## Obtaining
Precursor Apparatus is obtained by placing one of each [[Professor Robot|robot piece]] in the crafting grid.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Electron Transmitter]] 1x [[Superlite Motor]] 1x [[FTX 3070]] 1x [[Robotron Reflector]] 1x [[Control Switch]] 1x [[Synthetic Heart]]|Recipe: [[Control Switch]] + [[Electron Transmitter]] + [[FTX 3070]] + [[Robotron Reflector]] + [[Superlite Motor]] + [[Synthetic Heart]] → Precursor Apparatus|

# Precursor Gear
Aliases: Ancient, Ancient Reforge, Ancient reforge
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones, Catacombs Loot, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Precursor Gear]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Ancient
Applied To: [[Armor]]
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXX (30) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PRECURSOR_GEAR
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Ancient reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Health  Defense  Strength  Crit Chance  Crit Damage  Intelligence; Ancient Bonus Grants +1 Crit Damage per Catacombs skill level you have.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 30.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Precursor Gear is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Ancient]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]]. It grants [[Health]], [[Defense]], [[Strength]], [[Intelligence]], and [[Crit Chance]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
Precursor Gear can be obtained from Gold, Diamond, Emerald, Obsidian, and Bedrock [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] and its [[Catacombs|Master Mode]] variant.
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|

|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Diamond|500,000 [[Coins]]|6.6211% (1 in 15.1 runs)|8.4312% (1 in 11.9 runs)|140|8|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Emerald|500,000 [[Coins]]|7.4498% (1 in 13.4 runs)|9.3084% (1 in 10.7 runs)|140|7|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Obsidian|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|10.6103% (1 in 9.4 runs)|14.6989% (1 in 6.8 runs)|140|16|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Bedrock|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|20.6952% (1 in 4.8 runs)|24.7179% (1 in 4 runs)|140|1200|

|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Diamond|500,000 [[Coins]]|7.819% (1 in 12.8 runs)|11.3657% (1 in 8.8 runs)|140|8|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Emerald|500,000 [[Coins]]|7.2293% (1 in 13.8 runs)|10.8522% (1 in 9.2 runs)|140|7|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|7.0973% (1 in 14.1 runs)|10.2974% (1 in 9.7 runs)|140|16|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|2,000,000 [[Coins]]|23.9939% (1 in 4.2 runs)|27.8164% (1 in 3.6 runs)|140|1200|
## Usage
### Reforging
Precursor Gear can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Ancient]] reforge, to grant [[Health]], [[Defense]], [[Strength]], [[Intelligence]], and [[Crit Chance]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXX (30)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Ancient]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Ancient Bonus, which grants 1 [[Crit Damage]] per player's [[Catacombs]] level.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|

|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|

The Prince Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Reborn]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Prince Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Prince]] with enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm Chance|Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Prince Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||

[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Prince Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Reborn]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Reborn ([[Attributes]]); Killing a [[Prince]] has a +10%–100% chance to add 1 Bonus Score ([[Dungeon Score]]) to your Dungeon Run.; Limit of +1 Score per run
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
