# Puffercloud
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Confirmations needed, Mutations
[Puffercloud]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PUFFERCLOUD
Tooltip: Puffercloud looks harmless, but step too close and it releases a toxic cloud. Probably best to stay away.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; EPIC MUTATION
A Puffercloud is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Puffercloud can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 6 [[Do-not-eat-shroom]] and 2 [[Snoozling]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Puffercloud needs water and has 14 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it increases yield by 30% and reduces water by -30%.
A Puffercloud releases spores as it grows, dealing 4%[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] of a player's max [[Health]] as [[True Damage]] to players standing within a 3x3x3 area of a Puffercloud.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Pufferclouds also drop 570 (8 stacks plus 58) [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]], 570 [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]], 1,200 (18 stacks plus 48) [[Moonflower]], 1,200 [[Sunflower]], and +9,600 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Puffercloud can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 500 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Puffercloud grants an additional 1,250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Pufferclouds are used to create a [[Devourer]].

- Puffercloud in the Greenhouse Puffercloud in the [[Greenhouse]]

[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PUNCHCARD_ARTIFACT
Tooltip: Rift Time: +30s; Ability: Punch in! LEFT CLICK Punch another player while this is in your inventory to both regain +25 Rift Time.; Usable once per player per rift visit up to 20 players.; EPIC ACCESSORY
The Punchcard Artifact is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]].
## Obtaining
The Punchcard Artifact can be bid on at [[Barrier Street]]. The top 25 bidders win the item.
## Usage
Accessory:
Punchcard Artifact [[Rift Time]]: +30
[Effects]
[Ability: Punch in! LEFT CLICK; Punch another player while this is in your inventory to both regain +25 Rift Time.; Usable once per player per rift visit up to 20 players.]
The Punchcard Artifact allows the player to punch other players to regain +25 [[Rift Time]].
### Game Message
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Punched someone|PUNCHCARD! You punched <player> and both regained +25ф Rift Time!|
|Punched by someone|PUNCHED! You were punched by <player> and both regained +25ф Rift Time!|
|Punched 20 players in a session|UH OH! You reached the limit of 20 players you can punch in one session!|
|Punched the same player after rejoining a session|AWKWARD! This player has already been punched by you... somehow!|
## Trivia
- Punching is a feature in Hypixel Lobbies, allowing players to hit staff members, making them fly in the air.
- It has the same texture as the [[9f™ Network|9f™ Operator Chip]].
- While you can only punch 20 players per rift visit, there is seemingly no limit to how many players can punch you.
  - In theory, this would allow for infinite [[Rift Time]].

# Pure Mithril
Aliases: Mithraic
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones, Dwarven Mines, Forged items
It takes a few hours of forging to purify Mithril - the impurities extract themselves while the Mithril coalesces.
[Pure Mithril]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Heart of the Mountain III (3) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])
Tooltip Text: It takes a few hours of forging to purify Mithril - the impurities extract themselves while the Mithril coalesces.
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Mithraic
Applied To: Mining Tools ([[Tools]])
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XVIII (18) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 51,200x (800 stacks (51,200 items total)) [[Mithril]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PURE_MITHRIL
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Mithraic reforge when combined with a Mining Tool.; It takes a few hours of forging to purify Mithril - the impurities extract themselves while the Mithril coalesces.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Fortune; Mithraic Bonus Grants +50 Mining Fortune towards Mithril and Titanium.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 18.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Pure Mithril is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Mithraic]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Mining Fortune]].
## Obtaining
The Pure Mithril can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]]. It requires 2 [[Refined Mithril]] and 6 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Pure Mithril]] 1 Pure Mithril
  - 2 [[Refined Mithril]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Mithril]]
## Usage
### Reforging
Pure Mithril can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Mithraic]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Fortune]], which scales with the Mining Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XVIII (18)]]. A Mining Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Mithraic]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Mithraic Bonus, which grants an additional 50 [[Mining Fortune]] for [[Mithril]] and [[Titanium]].
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|

|Reforge Bonus|Mithraic Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Fortune towards Mithril or Titanium.|Mithraic Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Fortune towards Mithril or Titanium.|Mithraic Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Fortune towards Mithril or Titanium.|Mithraic Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Fortune towards Mithril or Titanium.|Mithraic Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Fortune towards Mithril or Titanium.|Mithraic Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Fortune towards Mithril or Titanium.|Mithraic Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Fortune towards Mithril or Titanium.|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|

Tooltip: Honey Refill I Honeyhives have a +0.5% chance to refill instantly.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 15.; EPIC FOREST SHARD (ID E49)
The Queen Bee Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Honey Refill]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Honeycomb VII (7) ([[Honeycomb]])|16x [[Enchanted Honeycomb]] 16x [[Enchanted Egg]] 64x [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Honeycomb]] x4 ×4 + [[Enchanted Helix Log]] x16 ×4 + [[Enchanted Egg]] x16 → Queen Bee Shard|
### Honeyhive
A Queen Bee Shard has a 2.08% (1 in 48 rolls) chance to be obtained from a [[Honeyhives|Honeyhive]].
### Fusion
A Queen Bee Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Queen Bee Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Honey Refill]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Honey Refill ([[Attributes]]); [[Honeyhives]] have a +0.5%–5% chance to refill instantly.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

The Rabbit Godmother Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Divine Rabbit Luck]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Rabbit Godmother Shard can be purchased from the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]] for 20,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]] after reaching [[Chocolate Factory]] level 5. The player can purchase up to 6 each [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]].
### Fusion
A Rabbit Godmother Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Rabbit Godmother Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Divine Rabbit Luck]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Divine Rabbit Luck ([[Attributes]]); Increases your chance of finding DIVINE Rabbits during [[Hoppity's Hunt]] by +1%–10%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Rainbow Feather
Categories: Epic items, Items

Type: Pet Consumable

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Use on a Pet to increase its current level by 1.; Right-click on your summoned pet to use!; EPIC
A Rainbow Feather is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pets|Pet]] Consumable.
## Obtaining
A Rainbow Feather has a guaranteed chance to drop when [[Hunting]] a [[Critter Safari|SPARKLING]] [[Critters|Critter]] in the [[Critter Safari]]. The [[Attributes|Splendor]] attribute grants a +2-25% chance to drop a bonus Rainbow Feather when enabled.
## Usage
A Rainbow Feather can be used by right-clicking on a summoned [[Pets|Pet]] to grant it +1 level. The item is consumed on use.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Pet is at max level|Your pet is already at level 100!|
|Pet is at max level|Your pet is already at level 200!|
## Trivia
- This item may have been inspired by the Pokémon item of the same name, formerly known as the Rainbow Wing.
  - Rainbow Feather in SkyBlock also shares its functionality with the Rare Candy from the games as well.
- While this item appeared on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], it was a consumable item that granted Pets 1 [[Magic Find]] up to 5 times.

Item ID: RANCHERS_BOOTS
Tooltip: Health: +100; Defense: +70; Speed: +50; Gemstones: [] []; These boots gain +2 Defense and +4 Speed for every Farming Skill level you have. While on The Garden, these boots also gain +1 Farming Fortune for every Farming Skill level.; Ability: Farmer's Speed LEFT CLICK Set a maximum amount of  Speed.; Ability: Farmer's Grace Grants immunity to trampling crops.; Bonus Defense: 0; Bonus Speed: 0; Current Speed Cap: 400; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Farming Skill 21.; EPIC BOOTS
Rancher's Boots are [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Boots]] unlocked at [[Pumpkin|Pumpkin XI (11)]]. They grant [[Defense]] and [[Speed]] according to [[Farming]] level and allow their wearer to limit their speed.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Pumpkin XI (11) ([[Pumpkin]])|1x [[Farmer Boots]] 16x [[Polished Pumpkin]]|Recipe: [[Polished Pumpkin]] x2 ×8 + [[Farmer Boots]] → Rancher's Boots|
## Usage
Boots:
Rancher's Boots [[Health]]: +100 [[Defense]]: +70 [[Speed]]: +50
[Effects]
[These boots gain +2 Defense and +4 Speed for every Farming Skill level you have. While on The Garden, these boots also gain +1 Farming Fortune for every Farming Skill level. Ability: Farmer's Speed LEFT CLICK; Set a maximum amount of Speed. Ability: Farmer's Grace; Grants immunity to trampling crops.]
For every [[Farming]] level, the Rancher's Boots grant +2 [[Defense]] and +4 [[Speed]], as well as +1 [[Farming Fortune]] while on [[The Garden]]. The Rancher's Boots also grant several quality-of-life features while worn:
- Farmer's Speed allows the player to set their [[Speed]] cap while wearing Rancher's Boots. Specific crops are most efficiently farmed at specific speeds to ensure the optimal blocks broken per second.
  - While wearing Rancher's Boots, /setmaxspeed can be used to easily modify this without having to hold the item.
  - The [[Sundial]] serves the same purpose without taking up an armor slot.
- Farmer's Grace prevents crops from being trampled when jumping on them.
## Trivia
- [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]]'s Unlimited Speed perk disabled the speed cap modifying ability of Rancher's Boots.
- The Rancher's Boots is one of 9 armor pieces which have the darkest color with a hex color code of ⁠ #000000. The others are [[Bat Person Armor]], [[Obsidian Chestplate]], [[Squid Boots]], [[Tarantula Armor]], [[Shadow Assassin Armor]], [[Necromancer Lord Armor|Necromancer Lord Chestplate]], [[Snorkeling Armor]] and [[Wither Armor]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 7, 2024|The speed cap on Rancher's Boots can now be set up to 1,000.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 3, 2020|Added Rancher's Boots.|
|Sep 1, 2021|Newly-crafted Rancher's Boots are now black (#000000) instead of inheriting the color from [[Farmer Boots]].|
|Feb 14, 2023|0.18|Rancher's Boots now grant [[Farming Fortune]] while on [[The Garden]].|
|Feb 14, 2023|0.18|Rancher's Boots now requires Farming XXI (21) ([[Farming]]).|
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Rancher's Boots can no longer increase your speed cap beyond what you would have if you weren't wearing them.|
|Mar 12, 2024|0.19.12|The speed cap on Rancher's Boots can now be set up to 1,000 regardless of the player's current speed cap.|
|Unknown Date|Changed the minimum speed cap on the Rancher's Boots from 5 to 20.|
|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|The speed can now be set by the /setmaxspeed command.|
|Nov 19, 2024|0.20.8|The /setmaxspeed command can now take an argument to directly set the speed without opening the sign GUI.|

# Recall Potion
Categories: Epic items, Items, Pages with UIs, Tools, Mining, Crystal Hollows
[Recall Potion]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Crystal Nucleus]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RECALL_POTION
Tooltip: A mystical and glowing elixir imbued with special abilities from another dimension.; Ability: Ancient Recall RIGHT CLICK When in the Crystal Hollows allows you to save and warp back to a saved location.; EPIC
Recall Potion is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
The Recall Potion can be obtained from the treasure bundle that is spawned after placing all [[Gemstone Crystals]] in the [[Crystal Nucleus]]. It has a 0.08244159% (1 in 1212.98) chance to be rolled for each drop in the treasure bundle.
## Usage
Right clicking will open an interface. The player can save a set of coordinates, or warp to a set of coordinates that has been saved. The item will be consumed upon warping to a location.
## Trivia
- The item's name and texture seems to be inspired by an item of the same name from Terraria. This is further supported by the item lore, "A mystical and glowing elixir imbued with special abilities from another dimension."
  - The ability on the other hand seems to be inspired by another Terraria potion, the Potion of Return.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Recall Potion.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Recall Potion.|
Linked from: [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Crystal Nucleus]], [[Pity and RNG Meters]], [[Potions (disambiguation)]], [[Shimmersparkle Chestplate]]

### Mob Drops
The Red Claw Egg is a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drop from Tier III or higher [[Sven Packmaster|Sven Packmasters]]. It requires [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer LVL V (5)]] to be able to drop.

- Tier III: 0.04% (1 in 2500)
- Tier IV: 0.12% (1 in 834)

Refined Diamond is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
The Refined Diamond can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It requires 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block|Enchanted Diamond Blocks]] and 8 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Refined Diamond]] 1 Refined Diamond
  - 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Diamond]]

2 Refined Diamond can be used to purchase a [[Perfectly Cut Diamond]] from [[Gemma]] in the [[Crystal Nucleus]].

Refined Mithril is an [[Rarity|EPIC]]. It is the refined form of [[Enchanted Mithril]].
## Obtaining
Refined Mithril can be forged in [[The Forge]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It requires 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] and 6 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Refined Mithril]] 1 Refined Mithril
  - 160 [[Enchanted Mithril]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Mithril]]

Refined Tungsten is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that can be obtained from refining in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
Refined Tungsten can be forged once the player has reached [[Tungsten|Tungsten III (3)]] and [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]]. It requires 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Tungsten]] and 1 hour to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Refined Tungsten]] 1 Refined Tungsten
  - 160 [[Enchanted Tungsten]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Tungsten]]
### Frozen Corpses
Refined Tungsten can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].
